# [Refactor] imports: Import names from where they are defined; remove re-export shims

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/47990) · state: closed · merged: True

## Original PR description

- Code imports each name from the module that defines it, or from the nearest module that exports it (`__all__`, a package `__init__`), instead of through a module that only imports it itself. 322 imports move, e.g. `GenerationBatchResult` from `sglang.srt.managers.utils` instead of `sglang.srt.managers.scheduler`, `ModelTaskType` from `configs.task_type` instead of `pipeline_configs.base`. A name a module binds per platform or as a fallback (inside a runtime `if` / `try`, such as `causal_conv1d_fn` in `mamba.py`) keeps going through that module.
- Imports a module kept only so others could import the name through it are removed, including the kernel forwards in `spec_utils` and `mem_cache.utils`, which the kernel layout already asks not to leave behind. The test that guarded the `spec_utils` forwards goes with them.
- Callers of the compatibility re-exports in `arg_groups.overrides`, `server_args` (`NS`, `A`, `Arg`, `LoRAPathAction`, and the unused `LoRARef`, `CudaGraphConfig`, LoRA constants and arg helpers), `model_runner`, `unified_radix_cache`, `sana_wm`, and the comparator's `output_types` import from the source, and the re-exports are removed. The choice lists and plugin helpers that `server_args` documents as public stay.
- Deprecated shims are removed, with every in-tree reference (code, tests, patch targets, docs, cookbook, scripts, workflows) moved to the real path:
  - `sglang.bench_serving` -> `sglang.benchmark.serving`
  - `sglang.bench_one_batch_server` -> `sglang.benchmark.one_batch_server`
  - `sglang.srt.utils.hf_transformers_utils` -> `sglang.srt.utils.hf_transformers`
  - `sglang.srt.layers.attention.nsa` -> `sglang.srt.layers.attention.dsa`
- `deepseek_janus_pro.py` imports the five names it uses from `configs.janus_pro` instead of `import *`.

No behavior change, except: the four module paths above no longer exist, and names this PR stops re-exporting can no longer be imported through the modules that used to forward them. Every moved import resolves to the same object as before.

Names no longer importable through the module that forwarded them, and where to import them from:

- `sglang.srt.arg_groups.overrides`: `MODEL_OVERRIDES`, `register_model_override`, `mamba_extra_buffer_of`, `_MODEL_OVERRIDE_FNS`, `_PREDICATE_OVERRIDE_FNS` -> `sglang.srt.arg_groups.model_override_base`
- `sglang.srt.server_args`:
  - `NS`, `A`, `Arg` -> `sglang.srt.arg_groups.arg_utils`
  - `LoRAPathAction` -> `sglang.srt.arg_groups.argparse_actions`
  - `LoRARef` -> `sglang.srt.lora.lora_registry`
  - `CudaGraphConfig`, `parse_cuda_graph_config_arg` -> `sglang.srt.model_executor.cuda_graph_config`
  - `LORA_TARGET_ALL_MODULES`, `SUPPORTED_LORA_TARGET_MODULES`, `human_readable_int`, `json_list_type`, `nullable_str` -> `sglang.srt.utils.common`
- `sglang.srt.mem_cache.unified_radix_cache`: `UnifiedTreeNode`, `UnifiedLRUList`, `UnifiedTreeCore` -> `sglang.srt.mem_cache.unified_cache.unified_tree_core`
- `sglang.srt.model_executor.model_runner`: `CHUNKED_PREFIX_CACHE_SUPPORTED_ATTENTION_BACKENDS`, `add_chunked_prefix_cache_attention_backend` -> `sglang.srt.arg_groups.choices` (still re-exported by `sglang.srt.server_args`)
- `sglang.srt.speculative.spec_utils`, `sglang.srt.mem_cache.utils`: the Triton kernel forwards -> their `sglang.kernels.ops.*` modules
- `sglang.multimodal_gen.runtime.models.dits.sana_wm`: its private helpers -> `sana_wm_components`
